## 🤔 What is Seedance 2.5?

**Seedance 2.5** is the next-generation video generation model from **ByteDance**, available via **Volcano Ark (火山方舟)**. The prompts in this repository are an **official preview/sample set** published by ByteDance / Volcano Ark to showcase Seedance 2.5's capabilities.

> ⚠️ **Seedance 2.5 is still rolling out and not yet generally available.** These are official preview prompts, **not community contributions**. Community-submitted prompts will be added here after general availability.

**Highlights showcased in this preview:**

* 🎬 **Native long-form generation** — extended single-shot durations
* 🖼️ **Multi-reference input** — many reference images per generation
* 📐 **Up to 4K resolution**
* 🔊 **Auto dubbing & scoring** — automatic voiceover and background music
* 🎨 **Creative text animation, UGC, and product showcases**

### Create a 30 second photorealistic documentary style video in 1:1 aspect ratio…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 30 second photorealistic documentary style video in 1:1 aspect ratio, from a Japanese point of view, set in the early 1950s in Japan. The video should feel like authentic postwar Japanese documentary footage, as if filmed by a local reporter visiting Hiroshima and Nagasaki several years after the atomic bombings. Use black and white or slightly faded monochrome tones, soft contrast, visible film grain, light dust, minor scratches, subtle flicker, gentle handheld camera shake and realistic 1950s lens softness. No modern objects, no modern buildings, no text and no logos. The concept is a quiet spot review documentary. A Japanese male reporter in his late 30s walks through the preserved bombing areas, ruins, memorial remains and surrounding streets. He wears simple 1950s Japanese clothing, a dark jacket, plain trousers and worn leather shoes. His face should look serious, thoughtful and respectful. He speaks directly to camera in a calm documentary tone, as if showing the audience what remains of the bombing sites. The first part takes place in Hiroshima. Show broken walls, damaged foundations, memorial remains, open land, modest postwar reconstruction, bicycles, a few pedestrians and sparse trees. The reporter slowly walks through the site, looking around carefully. He gestures toward the remains and speaks quietly. Japanese dialogue “ここが広島の爆心地です。” “今も傷あとが残っています。” “静かですが、とても重い場所です。” The second part transitions naturally to Nagasaki. Show a different landscape with surviving ruins, damaged stone structures, memorial ground, hills in the distance and early postwar rebuilding. The reporter stands still for a moment, then continues walking and observing the surroundings. Japanese dialogue “こちらは長崎です。” “広島とはまた違う空気があります。” “ここに来ると、戦争の恐ろしさを強く感じます。” The final moments are reflective and emotional, not dramatic. The reporter faces the camera one last time while the background remains quiet and still. Japanese dialogue “この記憶を忘れてはいけません。” “二度と繰り返してはいけないと思います。” The camera style should feel like real 1950s Japanese documentary filmmaking. Slow pans, natural walking shots, simple compositions, respectful framing and realistic environmental detail. Focus on atmosphere, ruins, silence, human memory and postwar recovery. Keep the performance understated and natural. Audio should feel period accurate. Use outdoor ambience with light wind, distant footsteps, bicycle sounds, faint city movement and soft room tone like an old field recording. Add very subtle emotional documentary music underneath, using restrained strings, soft piano and minimal traditional Japanese tonal elements. Keep the music low so the reporter’s Japanese dialogue remains the main focus. Everything must feel historically grounded, realistic and respectful. No sensational action, no fantasy elements, no polished cinematic gloss and no exaggerated destruction. Preserve consistent character appearance, authentic 1950s Japan environment and believable documentary realism throughout.
```

### 30-Second One-Take Prompt | Royal Wedding at the Edge of a Volcano

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
30-Second One-Take Prompt | Royal Wedding at the Edge of a Volcano
Overall Description
A 30-second, uncut historical-fantasy epic long take. The most beautiful adult bride of the volcanic kingdom 1, 27 years old, with black hair arranged in a low bun; a few strands loosened by the hot wind rest against her face. Her features are refined yet natural, with realistic skin texture, subtle visible pores, and a trace of volcanic ash, without any beauty-filter look. She wears a heavy ivory royal wedding gown. The hem of the dress and veil are dusted with ash. She wears no elaborate jewelry, no crown, and carries no weapon.
She has just left a wedding swallowed by smoke. She must marry into a family she does not love in exchange for peace in her kingdom. She walks slowly across an obsidian bridge above a volcanic cliff, senses the presence of her off-screen lover, and looks back one last time. She does not run away. In the end, she walks toward the responsibility waiting for her.
The core of the image is not disaster spectacle, but a restrained farewell inside an immense and dangerous world: love makes her look back; the kingdom makes her move forward.
Fixed Spatial Relationships
Using the bride's initial walking direction as the reference:
Ahead: the obsidian bridge leading deeper into the volcanic palace. In the distance stand the enormous archway of the wedding hall and a motionless black ceremonial guard.
Behind: the direction from which the bride came, where the camera and the off-screen lover are located. The wedding guests, musicians, and ritual braziers are swallowed by volcanic smoke and remain indistinct.
Left: an uninterrupted black volcanic stone wall, monumental statues of past lava monarchs, and torn ceremonial banners.
Right: an unguarded volcanic crater. Hundreds of meters below, lava rolls slowly, steam rises, ash drifts, and deep red reflections create the primary warm light source.
Deep front-right: the bridge curves right into the palace, and the destination disappears from view.
The bride always walks slightly left of the bridge centerline, never near the volcanic edge.
The camera always remains behind her right shoulder or in the right-rear zone. It never crosses in front of her body and never circles around through the statue-wall side.
When she looks back, her off-screen lover remains fixed roughly 20-30 cm beyond the camera's right side from her point of view. The lover never enters frame: no body, hands, shadow, reflection, or recognizable trace.
Her look-back must follow real physical movement: eyes first, then head over the right shoulder, then a slight turn of the upper body. Her feet remain pointed toward the bridge ahead. Her body must leave; her emotion remains behind.
Camera Rules
The entire scene uses a slow, breathing cinematic handheld camera. It feels like a silent observer standing beside the lover, not a mechanical gimbal and not a shaky documentary camera.
Camera route: close right-rear tracking -> bride stops -> camera continues an almost imperceptible drift -> bride looks back -> camera slowly pulls away -> extreme close-up opens into a bust shot -> then an upper-body shot -> bride turns forward -> camera continues retreating -> bride walks alone into the right-curving volcanic palace.
Even when she stops, the camera retains nearly invisible breathing and a slow backward drift. No locked-off camera, fast push-ins, whip movements, orbiting, or cuts.
Shot Prompt
0s-4s | Contemplation
Begin from behind the bride's right shoulder, 50-70 cm from the back of her head and shoulder. Her low black bun, right ear, shoulder, and ivory wedding gown occupy nearly half the left side of frame. On the right, preserve the volcanic crater, dark-red lava, and the bridge curving toward the palace.
She walks with heavy but unhurried steps. The hot updraft pulls the long skirt backward before it slowly falls again; the veil moves softly through airborne ash. She is not afraid. She is thinking through a decision she has already known cannot be avoided. Her brow is only faintly tense, her eyes remain fixed on the distant hall, her lower eyelids are slightly tight, her lips naturally closed, her jaw gently held, and her breathing deeper than normal.
4s-8s | Memory Suddenly Returns
The bride gradually slows down and stops. She must not freeze abruptly. Physical sequence: shorter stride -> heel settles on the obsidian -> center of gravity shifts slightly forward -> skirt continues one last swing from inertia -> heavy veil slowly settles.
The camera does not stop sharply with her. It drifts forward another few centimeters, then naturally slows. Her body remains facing the palace. Only then does her gaze change: her pupils shift almost imperceptibly toward the right rear; her brow does not tighten further but pauses for a tiny instant; her nostrils take a small breath; her lips part by less than a millimeter; her throat makes one faint swallow.
A low volcanic rumble carries in the distance. Ash occasionally crosses the bridge. The ceremonial firelight from the main hall flickers continuously through the smoke.
8s-13s | She Knows It Is Him
The bride slowly turns her head over her right shoulder. The movement must follow real anatomy: eyes -> head -> neck -> right shoulder -> upper body. Her feet remain pointed toward the bridge ahead.
At the same time, the camera drifts very slowly toward the left rear, allowing two-thirds of the right side of her face to enter frame while still remaining in her right-rear zone. Lava reflections rise softly from below on her right, while distant cool-white ceremonial light from the palace rim-lights her hair from the left front. The two light sources meet naturally across her face.
She finally sees her off-screen lover. Her first reaction is not a smile, but recognition: her pupils pause, her upper eyelids lift by the smallest degree, her breathing stops for less than half a second, fine lines between her brows gradually relax, and her expression moves from judgment into recognition.
13s-18s | A Smile Only for Them
The camera keeps retreating slowly, allowing the image to ease from an extreme close-up into a wider close shot. Once she realizes the lover is still there, her emotion changes almost invisibly.
Her mouth does not smile first. Her eyes soften first; her lower eyelids lift slightly; faint natural lines appear at the outer corners of her eyes; the inner brow relaxes; her previously held jaw releases completely. Only then does the corner of her mouth rise by the smallest amount.
It is a brief expression meant only for the person outside the frame. Her eyes hold love, memory, apology, and reluctance at once. Hot wind passes through her veil. Her wedding skirt trails in a pale ash-colored arc across the obsidian bridge. The smile lasts only a moment because she knows she cannot stay.
18s-23s | Accepting Fate
The camera continues its slow retreat, opening into a medium close-up above her chest. On the left, enormous statues of former lava monarchs enter the frame: black stone figures edged by dark-red firelight, their cold faces watching over the bridge. On the right, the crater, lava, and steam gradually open up. The bride becomes the center of the composition.
Her smile fades on its own. Do not make her expression change suddenly: the corners of her mouth return to neutral first, then the softness in her gaze gradually recedes. Her pupils briefly lower to the ash-stained fabric of her gown, then rise again. She takes a slow breath; her chest lifts slightly, and her shoulders settle back into place.
The faint tension between her brows returns, but it no longer means hesitation. It means acceptance. She finally admits that some marriages are not for love, some people must be left behind, and some costs can only be carried by her.
23s-27s | Becoming the Queen
The camera continues to retreat very slowly. The bride is now fully centered in frame: past lava monarch statues on the left, the volcanic crater and rolling red light on the right, the smoke-covered wedding behind her, and the right-curving obsidian bridge leading toward the palace entrance ahead.
She looks at her lover one final time. She slowly exhales, and her shoulders lower with the breath. Then her jaw steadies again; her back gradually straightens; her chest opens; her upper eyelids lower slightly; her pupils stop wandering; her lips close completely. She may give one nearly imperceptible nod.
At this moment, the ordinary woman disappears. What remains is the future queen walking toward the throne.
27s-30s | Walking into the Fire
She withdraws her gaze. Movement order: eyes leave the lover first -> look toward the palace bridge at the front-right -> head slowly turns back -> neck returns to center -> right shoulder moves forward -> upper body squares forward -> weight shifts ahead -> first step.
No sharp head turn. No anger. No looking back. She resumes walking toward the front-right. Her pace is not fast, but it is steadier than at the beginning. The camera does not chase her. It continues retreating at an extremely slow pace, allowing the bride to pull away from it.
Her ivory gown moves softly between the cold-black statues on the left and the deep-red lava on the right. In the final second, she is still walking. The bridge curves right into the smoke-covered palace. The future cannot be seen, but she has made her choice. Do not cut to black. End while she is still walking toward fate.
One-Take Continuity Constraints
The bride remains the same beautiful adult woman throughout: low black bun, natural realistic skin, ivory wedding gown, ash
```

### 参考@image1角色（正面、三四分、背面与剑的完整细节）：蒙眼布、白色长袍与所有褶皱、深V领口、金色胸饰与红宝石、金色腰带与装饰、金色脚环、赤足凉鞋、长剑与剑…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
参考@image1角色（正面、三四分、背面与剑的完整细节）：蒙眼布、白色长袍与所有褶皱、深V领口、金色胸饰与红宝石、金色腰带与装饰、金色脚环、赤足凉鞋、长剑与剑柄上的天平盘及链条。完全按照该参考图呈现人物五官、发型、发色、蒙眼布材质与绑法、白袍布料重量与褶皱、剑身、剑柄、天平盘、链条与所有装饰细节，不进行任何重新设计。

参考@image2场景：白色拱顶大厅 / Cathedral of Light，高大彩色玻璃窗、正午体积光、彩虹光柱（红、琥珀、翠绿、钴蓝、紫罗兰）投射在白色大理石地面形成锐利几何色块，体积光与浮尘。完全按照该参考图呈现建筑结构、光线方向与色彩，不进行任何重新设计。

30秒3D渲染电影质感，16:9，4K，24fps。次世代CG材质精度——白袍真实布料重量与褶皱、天平金属反射与链条物理晃动、剑刃光泽、蒙眼布纹理、皮肤次表面散射、体积光与浮尘、变形宽银幕横向耀斑、180度快门自然运动模糊、浅景深。

【天平设定】剑柄上的天平为可动物理物件，两侧盘面可上下倾斜，链条有真实重量、晃动与惯性。
【光线物理】彩色玻璃窗投射的彩虹光柱可被剑刃切开，切开后变成固态彩色玻璃碎片坠落并在大理石地面碎裂，带真实折射与碎裂轨迹。
【破坏累积】从0秒起彩色玻璃窗开始碎裂，碎片在空中飞行并逐步重新组装回原位，破坏状态只增不减、绝不重置。
【无缝转场】每2秒的机位变化必须由画面内元素（剑尖轨迹、布料飘动、碎片飞散、天平晃动、光柱切割）带动完成，绝不硬切、绝不黑场，镜头运动全程平滑连续。
【动作原则】所有挥剑动作必须优雅、流畅、如舞蹈般美丽。动作有预备、发力、跟随与过冲；次级动态（白袍大袖、下摆、长发、天平链条）滞后主体3至5帧且各自独立；重心真实转移，杜绝任何僵硬或不自然的关节角度。每一刀都令人屏息。

0-2秒
35mm极低角度仰拍，缓慢升起并由她抬剑的动作自然带动镜头上移。她静立于大厅中央，白袍自然下垂。她以极优雅的动作缓缓举起长剑，天平盘开始接住从彩色玻璃倾泻的彩虹光柱，一侧盘面缓缓下沉，链条轻微晃动。

2-4秒
镜头由剑刃切开第一道光柱的动作自然转为50mm侧向缓慢环绕。她以极慢、极美的动作挥出第一刀，转腕带真实惯性，白袍大袖与下摆随动作展开并滞后主体飘动。剑刃切开第一道彩虹光柱，光柱如固态彩色玻璃般碎裂，碎片带着折射光旋转坠落。

4-6秒
碎片坠落轨迹自然带动镜头转为24mm超广角低角度缓慢推近。连续第二、第三刀，动作流畅如舞蹈，每一下都有明确的起势、发力与收势。天平盘随挥剑剧烈摇摆，链条拉直又松弛。更多光柱被切开，彩色玻璃窗开始出现裂纹并崩裂出第一批大碎片。

6-8秒
大碎片飞散轨迹自然带动镜头转为85mm贴身侧拍，平滑跟随剑尖。她以优雅的大开大合动作继续挥剑，每一步都轻盈而精准，重心从脚掌经腰胯传导至刀尖，白袍完全展开如翅膀并带真实布料重量。彩色玻璃窗大面积碎裂，碎片在空中缓慢旋转、发光，开始沿着原来的铅线位置重新组装。

8-10秒
剑舞轨迹与旋转光之漩涡自然带动镜头转为14mm超广角环绕轨道，平滑连续。剑舞达到高潮，她连续挥出多刀，每个动作都令人屏息。天平盘几乎触地又被优雅翻转拉回。光柱被持续切开，碎片与重组的玻璃同时存在，形成巨大旋转光之漩涡，整个大厅被彩色碎片轨迹完全填满。

10-14秒
光之漩涡的旋转自然带动镜头转为35mm环绕并缓慢上升，展示全身。她继续以极致优雅的剑舞穿越光之漩涡，每一个转身、每一次收刀都精准而迷人，次级动态（袍袖、发丝、链条）滞后主体并各自独立。碎片重组加速，同时新的光柱仍被切开。整个画面被彩色光、碎片、体积光与剑气轨迹完全填满，空间被最大化利用。

14-18秒
她刻意控制天平摇摆的动作自然带动镜头转为50mm跟随，轻微速度斜坡。她突然加速，连续施展一连串极快却依然优雅的连续斩击，剑刃在空中留下多道持续的彩色刀痕。天平盘剧烈摇摆至极限，链条几乎绷直。碎片重组与光柱切割同时达到峰值，整个大厅变成光与碎片的风暴。

18-22秒
连续斩击的余势自然带动镜头转为24mm低角度仰拍并快速环绕半圈。她以一个华丽的大回旋收刀接一个极低的滑步，白袍大幅展开后急速回落，天平盘被她用手腕精准控制回到接近平衡的位置。残余碎片仍在高速旋转，光柱被最后几刀彻底切开成漫天碎光。

22-26秒
滑步与回旋的动作自然带动镜头转为85mm贴身跟随并缓慢上升。她突然向上纵身，在空中完成一次完整的优雅旋转斩击，剑刃划出巨大的彩色圆弧，天平盘在空中剧烈晃动。落地时单膝微屈卸力，白袍与长发滞后飘落，整个空间被最后一波高密度碎片与体积光完全吞没。

26-28秒
落地后的余韵自然带动镜头转为50mm侧脸中景，缓慢推近。她完成最后一次极慢而完美的收刀，天平盘缓缓趋于完全平衡。大部分碎片已归位，彩色玻璃窗接近恢复完整。白袍微微飘动后逐渐静止。

28-30秒

A pale reflective strap crosses her back diagonally and a coiled line crosses the other way, the two forming an X on her back. Image 2's front view omits both, so the rear views govern. Present every second, front on shots included. Headlamp lit throughout. Only one of her in the entire film. Image 1 draws her six times as a storyboard convention. No clones, no doubles, no second figure in the reflections.

CAMERA. One 30 second take, zero cut points, the camera never stops, she always drives toward frame right. The camera stays perpendicular to the line of travel, shooting from the side the way image 1 is drawn, so landmarks enter one side of frame, are passed, and are thrown out the other. Strong parallax, at least six. Never shoot down the tunnel's own depth axis. No locked off camera. No struggling on the spot. The water only rises, never falls.

REFERENCES. Image 1 gives spatial layout and landmark order only, it is hand drawn watercolour and is not a style reference. Image 2 gives face, build, clothing, kit. Image 3 gives light and grade only, not composition and not content.

BANNED. Every surface is bare: concrete, water stain, rust, cable. No numbers, circles, glowing number plates, white paint marks or signs. No cuts, slow motion, speed ramps. No locked off camera, no running on the spot, no coming to a stop, no turning to look at the camera at the end, no second protagonist. No text, letters, digits, nameplates, logos, flags or insignia. No dashed path lines, arrows, numbered circles, minimap, timeline or handwriting. No subtitles, no watermark. No 3D or CG look.

BEATS

0 to 5s. She drops from a ruptured pipe mouth in the left wall into ankle deep water and immediately runs right, boots throwing sheets of spray. Low side angle tracking laterally, the pipe mouth thrown out of frame left, the strap and coiled line clearly visible. She must displace visibly against the background, the conduits and light strips keep receding behind her. No running on the spot. Sound: spray, hard breathing, a low rumble far off.

5 to 10s. Water to the knee. The run degrades into a wade. She shoulders through a bank of floating steel lockers, ceiling conduits sweeping past one after another. No writing, numbers or nameplates on the lockers or walls. Sound: the drag of water enters, metal knocking.

10 to 15s. Water to the waist. At the cross junction a waterfall comes down from the stairwell to street level. She holds both hands in front of her face and forces through the sheet, her body visibly pushed off line by the pressure. Tracking from the side through the water, the stairwell entering frame right and thrown out again. Sound: the waterfall roar covers everything.

15 to 20s. Water to the chest, both feet off the floor. She grabs a bundle of cable on the wall and hauls herself forward hand over hand, past a row of dead junction boxes. Boxes and wall bare, no numbers and no dials. Half submerged camera, the waterline cutting through the middle of frame. Sound: the cable pulls taut underwater in a long scrape, she gulps air, water runs past the ears without stopping. This beat is full.

20 to 26s. Ahead the shutter is down to a half metre gap at the surface. She takes a deep breath and goes fully under, both hands on the bottom edge, pulling herself through the gap. The only light underwater is her headlamp, and the amber strip on the far side glows through the water. The frame must not go black. The camera sinks under and follows her through, medium shot, her face and the strap on her back in the same frame. Sound: the quietest passage in the film, everything pulled away at once, faint muffled pressure and bubbles, clearly quieter than the beat before.

26 to 30s. She breaks the surface in the shaft at the end, cold white daylight falling from the open hatch above. She grabs the iron ladder and climbs, the camera tilting up with her. Sound: everything returns. Water, gulped air, the ladder ringing.

The one that matters most: a ban that only lives in the rules layer does not survive. Anything that has to hold gets repeated inside the beat it belongs to.

### 高品質アニメ映像。近未来ゴシック幻想。映画的な劇場アニメ戦闘映像。高密度3Dトゥーン／セルルックのみ。劇場版クラスのキーアニメーション、上質なコンポジット、透明…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
高品質アニメ映像。近未来ゴシック幻想。映画的な劇場アニメ戦闘映像。高密度3Dトゥーン／セルルックのみ。劇場版クラスのキーアニメーション、上質なコンポジット、透明感のある照明、高密度背景美術、重心と接地が読める連続動作を維持する。

【アニメーション方向】
爆発的で鋭いアクション。キレのあるタイミング。予備動作は最小限。即時加速。大きく明確な全身運動。強い接触反動。接触後の慣性を明瞭に残す。待機、長い構え、長いタメ、スローモーション、静止した見せカットを作らない。速さをカメラ移動だけで表現せず、人物自身の脚運びと床上の実移動を必ず見せる。

【参照の役割と優先順位】
<<<Image1>>>  は顔と人物同一性の最優先参照。顔立ち、年齢感、目、虹彩、まつ毛、頬、細い顎、前髪、角、顔周辺の描画テイストを参照する。
<<<Image2>>>  は衣装・全身デザイン・武器参照。<<<Image1>>>  と同じ人物の衣装、装飾、体型、全身シルエット、靴、一本だけの長槍、固有配色を参照する。
<<<Image3>>>  は尻尾参照。<<<Image1>>>  ・<<<Image2>>>  と同じ人物の、腰後方への接続位置、黒曜石紫の鱗、根元から細い先端へ自然に絞られる一本だけの尻尾、尾先の紫光を参照する。

参照間に差異がある場合の優先順位は、顔・目・虹彩・顎・年齢感・前髪・角は<<<Image1>>>  、衣装・装飾・体型・全身シルエット・長槍は<<<Image2>>>  、尻尾の接続位置・鱗・太さ・細い先端は<<<Image3>>>  。参照画像内の背景、文字、枠、複数パネル、説明図、UIは映像へコピーしない。

【主人公の同一性固定】
主人公は全編で同じ一人のドラゴン少女。<<<Image1>>>  の小さく繊細な卵形の顔、細い顎、淡い肌、小さな鼻と口、多層に輝く紫からアメジスト色の大きな瞳、繊細な上まつ毛、濃い紫黒の短いボブ、青紫の毛先反射、頭部左右の二本の湾曲した黒紫の角を全Cutで維持する。
<<<Image2>>>  の細身で均整の取れた体型、黒から深紫の高襟ゴシック衣装、白い胸元の布、細い金縁と鎖装飾、紫黒のケープと広い袖、暗色タイツ、黒い靴、一本だけの長槍を維持する。
<<<Image3>>>  の一本だけの有機的な鱗尾を腰後方へ連続してつなぎ、黒曜石紫の鱗から細い発光尾先まで維持する。

遠景や高速回転中も顔を別人化・成人化・単純化しない。寄りでは<<<Image1>>>  の目の間隔、虹彩模様、顎の細さ、前髪、角の位置関係に戻す。全編で同じ顔、目、髪、角、衣装、体型、槍、尻尾、固有配色を保つ。変化してよいのは表情、視線、呼吸、戦闘姿勢、髪・衣装・尻尾の自然な遅れだけ。

【敵の同一性固定】
敵は一体だけの巨大な有機的妖怪鬼。主人公の約二倍の体格、煤けた濃い灰紫の粗い皮膚、後方へ伸びる象牙色の二本角、暗赤く光る両目、牙のある明確な鬼の顔、長い黒い乱髪、破れた黒紫の羽織、正面中央で暗赤く発光する一つの呪紋を持つ。全Cutで同じ造形を維持し、結晶体、水晶体、無機物へ変化しない。
主人公と鬼は別個の造形。主人公の顔、目、髪、角、衣装、装備、体型、黒紫とアメジストの配色を鬼へ移さない。鬼の顔、目、髪、角、羽織、体格、灰紫と暗赤の配色を主人公へ移さない。特徴混合、顔平均化、衣装交換、クローン化を防ぎ、余分な人物を出さない。

【共有画風】
線画は細く繊細、線色は濃紺から青紫。顔、髪、衣装、角、尻尾、鬼へ明快な二段から三段のセルシェーディングを与え、透明感のある淡い中間影を一層残す。影は青紫と濃紺を中心にし、黒く潰さない。動作中もセル影境界を立体へ追従させ、フレームごとのちらつきや無関係な反転を起こさない。
配色は黒、深紫、アメジスト、控えめな金、柔らかな白。瞳と尾先は多層の紫光、髪は細い紫青の絹糸状ハイライト、肌は清潔でマットなアニメ調。白熱部は接触中心の狭い範囲だけに置き、その周囲へ高彩度のアメジストと黒紫の色面を広く残し、白飛びで細部を消さない。
布、金属、角、鱗、ガラスを異なる反射と粗さで高密度に描き分ける。高速部だけ方向性のあるモーションブラーを限定し、顔、両目、両手、穂先、石突の輪郭は読める鮮明さを保つ。
主人公の顔へ淡い暖色を含む柔らかな冷白キーライト。背景は低彩度の青灰色と白灰色へ整理し、シアンや青の環境光は補助に抑える。太い黒輪郭、簡略TVアニメ、平坦な単層セル塗り、低密度背景、汎用的な3D美少女顔、滑らかなプラスチックCG、半写実、写実、実写、くすんだ色、画風混合にしない。

【舞台・光】
舞台は巨大なガラス天井と上下二層の回廊を持つ近未来の戦闘アトリウム。白灰色の太い構造梁、淡い青空が透ける強化ガラス面、奥行きのある回廊、低彩度の機械設備、磨かれた淡い灰色の強化ガラス床。床は人物、鬼、槍、紫の魔力光を柔らかく反射する。
手前の梁、中層回廊、遠方のガラス壁を三段の奥行きに分け、カメラ移動へ明確なパララックスを出す。柱脚、接合金具、手すり、ガラス継ぎ目、床下フレームを精細に描くが、主役の輪郭と重なる場所では明度差を落として整理する。落下後も同じ建築の下層として梁の方向、光源、割れた開口部の上下関係を維持する。読める看板、文字、広告、ロゴは置かない。
ガラス天井からの冷白キーライトは全Cutで同じ方向を保ち、主人公の顔には弱い暖色フィル、輪郭には細い青紫リムライトを残す。
```

### Create a 15-second ultra-photorealistic travel vlog featuring the SAME young…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 15-second ultra-photorealistic travel vlog featuring the SAME young European woman throughout the entire video. She has natural skin texture, subtle facial imperfections, realistic hair movement, authentic body proportions, and completely natural expressions. The video must look like genuine footage recorded by a real travel creator, NOT AI-generated or cinematic CGI.

SHOT 1 — 0–3 seconds:
Selfie-style handheld footage. The woman walks naturally down a beautiful European old-town street in the morning. She holds a small vlog camera at arm's length, looks directly into the lens and gives a subtle genuine smile. Natural walking bounce, slight handheld shake, realistic breathing and hair movement. Background includes pedestrians casually walking, parked bicycles, small cafés, textured historic buildings and soft morning sunlight.

SHOT 2 — 3–6 seconds:
She turns the camera away from herself toward the street. A natural handheld pan reveals detailed European architecture, outdoor café tables, people having coffee, bicycles passing and a small bakery. The camera movement is imperfect and human, with slight exposure adjustment as it moves from shadow into sunlight.

SHOT 3 — 6–9 seconds:
Cut naturally back to selfie footage. She continues walking and briefly says something casually to the camera with natural lip movement and relaxed facial expressions. A light breeze moves a few strands of her hair. People pass behind her naturally without looking at the camera. Realistic depth, authentic street ambience and subtle camera shake.

SHOT 4 — 9–12 seconds:
POV-style footage from her camera as she enters a small local café. The camera moves naturally through the doorway toward a wooden table with a freshly prepared coffee and a small pastry. Realistic reflections on glass, steam rising subtly from the coffee, natural indoor lighting, background conversations and realistic movement.

SHOT 5 — 12–15 seconds:
She sits outside the café and turns the camera back toward herself. She takes a small sip of coffee, looks into the lens and smiles naturally. The camera remains handheld and slightly imperfect. Background pedestrians, bicycles and café activity continue moving naturally. End with a subtle realistic focus shift from her face toward the street behind her.

VISUAL REQUIREMENTS:
Absolute photorealism, real-world physics, realistic human motion, authentic handheld smartphone/vlog camera behavior, natural skin pores and texture, realistic eyes and hair, physically accurate lighting and shadows, natural exposure changes, realistic reflections, detailed European environment, believable background pedestrians, consistent face and clothing across every shot.

Avoid overly smooth camera movements, artificial beauty filters, plastic skin, exaggerated expressions, perfect symmetry, CGI appearance, dramatic movie-style transitions, unrealistic background motion, floating objects, warped hands, distorted faces or AI-looking details.

The final result should feel like an actual 15-second travel vlog filmed spontaneously by a real European woman on a high-quality handheld camera.
```

5–10s: She notices a tiny Japanese dessert shop, points toward it and says, “Look! That one!” She smiles excitedly while continuing forward. The boyfriend naturally adjusts the camera to keep her entire body in frame.

10–15s: She walks a few more steps, then turns back toward the camera with a warm, spontaneous smile. End naturally while she continues walking.

Style: Photorealistic authentic boyfriend vlog, medium-wide full-body framing, natural handheld shake, realistic walking movement, autofocus breathing, slight motion blur, natural Tokyo lighting and background activity, genuine expressions and accurate lip-sync.

Strict: One continuous take, no cuts, no transitions, no zoom-in, no cropping, no face/identity changes, no outfit changes, no CGI, no text, no logos, no commercial look.

### 参考@image1与@image2的人物、@image3的场景，严格按照参考图呈现，不进行任何重新设计。

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
参考@image1与@image2的人物、@image3的场景，严格按照参考图呈现，不进行任何重新设计。

【人物】@image1＝浅色米白与蓝色衣裙、黑发渐变蓝色、黄绿色眼睛的女性。@image2＝黑色华丽铠甲、左眼覆盖金色雕花半面具的黑发女性，力量为黑蓝色的光。五官、发色、服饰、铠甲与面具一律以各自参考图为准。
【场景】@image3＝哥特式图书馆，中央一张巨大的环形木桌（桌心为中空的圆形空洞），四周高耸书架与二层回廊，彩绘玻璃长窗将阳光折射成大片彩虹光柱洒在石地上。

15秒3D MMORPG级实时渲染电影质感，4K，16:9，24fps。次世代游戏CG的材质精度——铠甲金属的真实反射与磨损、布料织纹与重量、发丝根根分明、皮肤次表面散射、体积光与浮尘、屏幕空间反射。
【摄影】变形宽银幕镜头的横向耀斑与椭圆散景、180度快门运动模糊、浅景深且焦点始终跟随她的眼睛与双手；焦段在14mm超广角与100mm微距之间大幅跳变，绝不连续两次使用相近焦段；大量使用摇臂升降、越过栏杆的跨越运动、缓慢环绕轨道与推轨变焦。

【魔法表现·只用光，绝不出现魔法阵】全片不得出现任何魔法阵、符文圆环、几何法阵或悬浮文字；她的力量只以纯粹的光呈现——黑蓝色的强光自掌心内部透出并顺着手指缝隙溢散，掌心亮到能照亮她的下颌与面具；金色面具下的瞳孔透出同色强光；光越强，周围的环境光就被吸走越多。
【气场·全程可见并只增不减】黑蓝色的发光薄雾持续自她的肩线与长袍下摆渗出并向下流淌，落地后化作缓慢蔓延的暗色影子；她周围的浮尘违反重力向上飘升，而画面其他区域的浮尘正常下落；靠近她的彩虹光柱明显变暗、颜色被抽淡并朝她的方向弯折；随时间推进，暗域范围不断扩大，最终覆盖整层回廊。
【坐姿设定】@image2的坐姿必须显得慵懒、优雅而傲慢：深深靠进椅背，一条腿高高翘在另一条腿上，悬空的那只靴尖挂在栏杆外侧并随意地上下轻晃；一侧手肘懒散地撑在扶手上，另一只手托着摊开的书；下颌微抬，眼皮半垂，全身没有一丝紧绷。
【武器铁律】本段全程两人手中与身上都没有任何武器，也不佩戴任何刀鞘剑鞘。
【构图铁律】两人视线始终锁定对方，绝不出现背对背或各自望向反方向的构图。
【无缝转场·绝不硬切】每2秒的机位变化由画面内元素带动完成：镜头贴着彩虹光柱滑行、随翻动的书页掠过、沿书架侧面推移、被向上飘升的浮尘带向下一个角度。

0-2秒｜100mm微距起幅接50mm垂直上摇缓推：镜头自二层回廊栏杆外悬空的那只靴尖开始——靴尖正随意地上下轻晃；镜头沿翘起的腿线、垂落的深蓝长袍与铠甲护甲缓缓上移，掠过懒散撑在扶手上的手肘，最后停在她半垂着眼皮低头看书的侧脸；黑蓝色发光薄雾自她肩线渗出，周围浮尘反常地向上飘升。她一边看书一边开口说话，视线没有离开书页。
2-4秒｜85mm过肩浅景深接摇臂越过栏杆下降：她用指尖翻过一页并继续说话，语气轻慢；镜头越过她的肩头、跨过栏杆缓缓下降俯视一层——@image1立在敞开的拱门下正抬头望向二层。
4-6秒｜35mm缓慢环绕轨道自下而上：她慢条斯理地把翘着的腿放下、单手撑扶手起身，长发与长袍延迟数帧才落定；她把书随手搁在栏杆上，脚下的暗色影子随起身向四周扩散一圈。
6-8秒｜100mm贴手部横移跟拍：镜头紧贴她的手，跟随指尖一路轻抚过一排排书脊；黑蓝色的光自她的指缝间持续溢出并擦过每一本书的封面，所过之处书页自行翻动，暗色薄雾沿书架蔓延。
8-10秒｜100mm微距接快速后拉至50mm：她的指尖停在一本书上，缓缓抽出、垂眼扫过封面、随即干脆地推回原位；书本归位的瞬间，她掌心的黑蓝色强光骤然亮起一档并照亮整排书架。

12-14秒｜14mm随她一同垂直坠落的跟随镜头，落地瞬间镜头随冲击波急速后退：她自二层纵身跃下，双掌的黑蓝色强光拖出两道长长的光尾；她笔直落进环形木桌正中的圆形空洞，双脚触底的刹那，一道黑蓝色的强光与环形气浪自桌心同时炸开横扫整座大厅，石地被压出放射状裂纹，整排书架的书页在同一瞬间全部脱离并漫天飞散。

### One continuous aerial FPV shot, a single virtual lens throughout, no cuts.

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
One continuous aerial FPV shot, a single virtual lens throughout, no cuts.

The camera flies one unbroken path at speed: low behind the advancing armored giant, forward past
him to the lone youth, once around the youth, then out along the stone's flight to the back of the
giant's helmet, and finally up and over him as he falls. All scale changes come from camera and
subject movement only. The camera never stops moving and never hovers; it holds a stable horizon,
banks naturally through turns, and decelerates only where it arrives at a subject.

MAIN SUBJECT: a lean youth in a coarse undyed wool tunic, bare forearms, dust on his shins. A
leather sling hangs from his right hand: one cord ends in a loop already seated over his middle
finger, the other ends in a knot. A smooth pale stone is in his left hand.

OPPOSING SUBJECT: an enormous armored figure seen from behind throughout — bronze scale
back-plate, a tall crested helmet, shoulders twice the width of a man's, a long spear in his right
hand. He is walking forward. A shield-bearer half his height walks ahead of him carrying a tall
shield.

FAR ARMY, behind the giant: deep locked ranks of bronze, tall rectangular shields overlapping edge
to edge, every helmet carrying the same crest, a dense upright forest of spears, dark banners
standing out stiff in the wind. The ranks hold formation and do not move.

NEAR LINE, behind the youth: a single thin line of men in leather and undyed cloth, mismatched
gear, wide gaps between them, few spears and no banners. Some shift their weight; none step
forward.

SCENE: a dry riverbed cutting through a wide valley at midday; cracked pale mud near the far bank,
loose grey gravel through the middle, water-smoothed cobbles at the near end; limestone slopes
rising on both sides.

[0:00-0:03] The camera tears in low and fast over the giant's shoulders from behind, banking hard,
and rises just enough to clear his crest without slowing. His shoulders roll with each stride, the
spear swings at his side, and the shield-bearer scrambles to stay ahead of him. Beyond him the
locked bronze ranks hold the far slope, shields overlapping and banners stiff in the wind.
END STATE: the crested helmet and scale back-plate fill the frame, the massed army behind them.

[0:03-0:06] The camera surges past the giant at full speed down the length of the riverbed, then
decelerates hard and settles at the youth's eye level. He is walking up the cobbles toward the
giant, stone in his left hand, sling hanging from his right, and he stops as the camera arrives.
Far behind him the thin line of men stands still and watches.
END STATE: the youth stands alone facing forward, the giant's armored back looming beyond his
shoulder.

[0:06-0:13] The camera slows to a walking pace and carries one steady circle around the youth,
holding him centred while the valley turns behind him. He brings the sling's pouch up into his
left hand, drops the stone into it, closes the pouch around it, and pinches the knotted cord
between his thumb and forefinger. The loaded pouch swings down and both cords pull straight. Over
his shoulder the armored back and the shield-bearer grow steadily larger.
END STATE: the loaded pouch hangs at the bottom of its swing with both cords taut.

[0:13-0:17] The camera completes the circle at his shoulder, then breaks away and accelerates past
him, gaining speed with every rotation of the sling. His shoulders set and he swings the sling
beside his head in three flat rotations, each faster and wider than the last, lifting grit off the
gravel at his feet. His front foot plants hard, his hips turn through, and his thumb and
forefinger open; the knotted cord flies loose, the cords whip straight, and his arm follows
through across his body as his tunic snaps against his ribs.
END STATE: the stone is clear of his hand and travelling.

[0:17-0:22] The camera locks alongside the stone at speed and drives with it toward the crested
helmet. The stone stays in the lower centre of frame, close and sharply detailed, turning slowly,
while the gravel and the valley floor tear past beneath it in heavy motion blur; the ground slows
almost to a standstill with grit hanging in the air, then tears past again faster than before. The
helmet fills the frame.
END STATE: the stone strikes the back of the helmet and the crest whips over.

[0:22-0:30] The camera climbs up and over the giant as he falls and holds looking down on him. The
spear drops and clatters onto the stones, his arms fall slack, he stands upright and motionless
for one beat, then one knee folds and his whole weight pitches forward until the scale back-plate
lands flat against the riverbed floor and dust bursts outward across the cobbles. The
shield-bearer drops the tall shield and runs. On the far slope the front shields drop away and the
forest of spears tilts backward as the locked ranks give ground; on the near side the thin line
breaks forward with arms going up and runs down onto the riverbed.
END STATE: the giant lies face down on the riverbed with the two crowds streaming in opposite
directions past him.

LIGHT: hard midday sun from high right; short dense shadows; heat haze rising off the stones.

SOUND: rushing air, close and constant; the low mass of an army below; the rising whir of the
swinging sling, faster on each rotation, ending in a hard snap; the air drops to near silence
through the slowed beat; a spear clattering on stone; the heavy collapse of armor onto gravel;
then a rising roar from one side and falling voices from the other.

STYLE: shot on 35mm anamorphic, 2-perf, grounded photographic realism.
Anamorphic character throughout: subtle horizontal lens flare on the sun,
oval bokeh in the out-of-focus gravel, slight edge stretching at the frame
extremes, gentle barrel distortion on fast camera moves. Visible film grain,
halation blooming softly around the brightest highlights. Muted earth palette
of bone, ochre and olive; hard midday contrast with detail retained in the
shadows.

AVOID: no cuts, no camera teleporting or jumping, no hovering or drifting, no sliding or floating
figures, no body fusion, no duplicate or morphing crests, no extra limbs, the sling cords keep
believable weight and inertia throughout, no on-screen text, no watermark, no music.
```

12–15 sec: Return to the narrow wooden alley. She walks toward the camera holding her drink, then briefly turns and looks back with a soft smile. Camera slowly pulls backward, revealing the beautiful traditional Kyoto architecture as the scene fades naturally.

Overall style: photorealistic Japanese travel film, authentic Kyoto atmosphere, natural human motion, realistic walking physics, subtle breathing and blinking, detailed hair and fabric movement, warm golden-hour lighting, soft cinematic lens flare, shallow depth of field, natural handheld/gimbal camera movement, realistic shadows and reflections, documentary-style cinematography, 35mm film look, premium travel commercial, highly detailed, seamless transitions, no artificial-looking CGI, no distorted hands or face, consistent character appearance throughout.

### Generate a continuous 30-second raw live USA television news helicopter…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Generate a continuous 30-second raw live USA television news helicopter coverage of the escalating aftermath of a wrong-way freeway pursuit. Core subject is the same overweight elderly woman in a floral bathrobe, now driving a stolen police car at dangerous speed, who blasts through a police perimeter into an open airfield, fights off multiple officers with absurd ease, climbs into a parked police helicopter and steals it, taking off unsteadily while officers cling on and fall away, escaping into the sky. Style is unpolished live TV news helicopter footage only: continuous single high-altitude shot with aggressive tracking, natural organic shake, realistic physics with comedic timing, bright daylight, no cinematic grading, no slow motion, no film look. Tone is unintentionally hilarious live news chaos — the humor comes from the absurd contrast of a grandma in a bathrobe outfighting trained officers, not from cartoon physics.

阶段一
开始时：Distant high helicopter view continuing the pursuit. The stolen marked police car driven by the elderly woman in the floral bathrobe is speeding down the freeway, three fresh police cars in tight chase formation behind her, sirens blazing. Her bathrobe flutters out the driver's window.
主要事件：She swerves across all lanes, takes an exit ramp at reckless speed, clips a row of traffic cones sending them scattering, and smashes through a chain-link gate into a small municipal airfield where a police helicopter sits parked on a helipad, rotors slowly spinning on idle.
结束时：The stolen police car is skidding across the tarmac toward the parked helicopter, chase cars pouring through the broken gate behind her, ground officers near the helipad turning in confusion.
阶段二
承接上一阶段：Same continuous distant helicopter tracking shot, same woman, same stolen police car, same airfield.
主要事件：She brake-slides the police car to a crooked stop beside the helipad, bumping a stack of equipment crates which topple comically. She climbs out unhurried, adjusts her bathrobe with dignity, and starts waddling briskly toward the helicopter. Two ground officers sprint to intercept her, arms out.
结束时：The woman is meters from the helicopter, two officers closing in from opposite sides, more chase cars screeching to a stop across the tarmac.
阶段三
承接上一阶段：Continuous same high-angle news helicopter framing, identities and vehicles unchanged.
主要事件：The first officer grabs her arm — she counters instantly, flips him over her hip with absurd judo precision, and he lands flat on his back. The second officer hesitates mid-step, visibly reconsidering his life choices, then lunges anyway; she sidesteps and shoves him into the equipment crates, which collapse over him. A third officer arrives, slips on a rolling water bottle from the crates, and takes himself out without her touching him.
结束时：Three officers down around the helipad in increasingly undignified positions, the woman calmly climbing into the helicopter pilot seat, bathrobe caught briefly in the door before she yanks it in.
阶段四
承接上一阶段：Identical continuous helicopter shot, no cut, same people and vehicles.
主要事件：The helicopter rotors spin up fast. One determined officer sprints and grabs the landing skid just as it lifts off. The helicopter rises unsteadily, wobbling side to side like a first-time pilot, tilting dangerously as the officer dangles from the skid a few feet off the ground. She tilts the aircraft; he loses grip at low altitude and drops safely onto a parked squad car's hood, denting it and setting off its alarm. Officers below scramble, point, and shout; one throws his hat on the ground in frustration.
结束时：The helicopter is climbing away from the airfield, wobbling but gaining confidence, officers scattered across the tarmac below, one on the squad car hood, the news helicopter tracking the ascent.

阶段五
承接上一阶段：Same continuous distant high-altitude news helicopter tracking, same identity and aircraft.
主要事件：The stolen police helicopter levels out and accelerates away across the city skyline edge, flying just competently enough to be terrifying. It performs one slow, wobbly celebratory circle — almost a victory lap — as police cars stream uselessly along the roads far below trying to follow. Her floral bathrobe sleeve is visible waving once out the pilot window, an unmistakable taunt.
结束时：The stolen helicopter shrinks toward the horizon at full speed, a dozen police vehicles gridlocked and helpless on the streets below, the news helicopter holding its distant tracking shot on the escape.
保持一致：Maintain the exact same overweight elderly woman in the same floral bathrobe, the stolen marked police car transitioning to the stolen police helicopter, continuous single distant high-altitude helicopter camera with natural live-news shake and aggressive tracking, the same bright daylight, realistic vehicle and human physics with comedic timing (humor from absurd contrast, not cartoon physics), and zero cuts for the entire 30 seconds. No cinematic camera work, no slow motion, no dramatic color grade.
Audio: heavy continuous helicopter rotor from both aircraft, constant police sirens, tires screeching on tarmac, chain-link gate crash, crates toppling, body-fall thuds, car alarm blaring after the officer lands on the hood, rotor wash intensifying at takeoff, shouting officers, one frustrated yell, engine roar as the helicopter accelerates away. No music. No subtitles. No voice-over.
```

A woman (reference image provides only her face and hairstyle) sits alone at a cozy Korean neighborhood restaurant beside a large open window overlooking a quiet alley. She wears a relaxed cream linen short-sleeve blouse with shoulders fully covered and light blue jeans. She remains seated on the same side facing the camera throughout the entire video. No other faces appear; only a server's hands briefly enter the frame.
The wooden table holds a bubbling stone bowl of kimchi jjigae, a bowl of steamed rice, grilled seaweed, assorted banchan (kimchi, spinach, bean sprouts), stainless spoon and chopsticks, a glass of iced barley tea, and a folded napkin. Warm steam rises naturally while sunlight filters through the window, creating a cozy late-summer atmosphere.

와... 냄새 미쳤다.

진짜 맛있다.

이 조합 최고다.

She lifts a large piece of silky tofu dripping with spicy broth, tastes it slowly, nods with approval, and says:
국물이 끝내준다.

The meal is finished. Empty bowls remain on the table. She leans back, closes her eyes for a moment as a gentle breeze moves the curtains, then looks into the camera with a satisfied smile:
또 올게.

He reaches the edge and steps onto the left side of the large rotating circular sand platform.
00:04–00:08 — ROTATING SAND PLATFORM

00:08–00:12 — RISING HURDLES

00:12–00:16 — SWINGING DEFENDERS

00:26–00:29 — FINAL SHOT
Finish-side telephoto shot showing the spinning ring, goal and sand below. He plants his left foot and strikes the ball toward the open goal. The spinning ring continues; the goal stays fixed.
00:29–00:30 — ENDING
Ground-level close-up. He sits up on the sand, completely covered in fine white grains, looks at the missed goal, then laughs and pumps his fist once in good humor. Crowd cheers and laughs. Orange ring keeps rotating behind him.
AUDIO:
Fast Brazilian funk/samba game-show BGM, whistle, ball kicks, sand footsteps, mechanical arm whooshes, net climbing sounds, crowd reactions and ocean waves. Contestant: “Vamos pro gol!” Commentator: “Será que ele consegue?!” After miss: “Quaseee!” Accurate Brazilian Portuguese lip-sync; lower BGM during dialogue.
CONTINUITY:
Every shot begins exactly where the previous shot ends. No teleportation, skipped obstacles, backward movement or impossible actions. Same contestant, clothing, face and environment. Rotating sand platform always clockwise; spinning goal circle always rotates RIGHT. Ball physics remain consistent throughout.

Sound design: heavy rainfall, thunder, distant explosions, sirens, screaming crowds, footsteps splashing through water, crashing metal, deep cinematic bass, roaring waves, and an intense rising orchestral score that builds toward the final creature reveal.

Important: Maintain strict character consistency, realistic physics, natural human movement, realistic facial expressions, coherent geography between shots, and seamless cinematic transitions. Avoid cartoonish CGI, distorted faces, extra limbs, unnatural body movement, flickering objects, or inconsistent clothing.

LOCATION: Small Indian railway town, old family house, crowded railway platform, departing night train

MAIN CHARACTER: Male, 28, simple dark shirt, worn backpack, emotionally restrained, natural expressions

00:00–00:04 → Young man searches an old wooden cupboard. He pulls out his father's jacket, shakes off dust and discovers a faded railway ticket inside the pocket.

00:08–00:12 → He runs through a narrow street toward the station, dodging a bicycle, auto-rickshaw and pedestrians. Camera follows handheld from behind.

00:12–00:16 → He reaches the platform breathless. A train is already waiting. He looks at the ticket, then at the coach number.

00:16–00:20 → He boards the correct coach and searches the seats. An elderly passenger notices the ticket and quietly points toward an empty window seat.

00:20–00:24 → He sits down and discovers a handwritten note tucked behind the seat: “If you ever find this, take the journey I never finished.”

00:24–00:28 → Train begins moving. He looks through the window toward the town disappearing behind him, quietly emotional but not crying dramatically.

AUDIO: Street traffic, footsteps, breathing, station announcements, train brakes, carriage vibration, paper rustling. No overpowering music.

REALISM: Natural Indian accents and body language, realistic crowd density, imperfect human reactions, authentic railway sounds, no glossy commercial appearance, no artificial skin, no CGI-looking faces.

She wears a white sleeveless blouse with black polka dots over a white undershirt, and a black smartwatch on her left wrist.

She starts by looking down at her hands held near her chest, fingers gently pinched as if holding an invisible frame. She then raises both hands to the sides of her face, forming an L-shape / finger-frame gesture with index fingers and thumbs, creating a rectangular portal in front of her face. Through the finger-frame portal, her face and upper body smoothly transition into a slightly more stylized, anime-influenced version of herself while keeping the same hairstyle and neutral expression. She lowers her hands, returning to the original realistic appearance, and makes a small heart shape with her fingers near her chest while giving a soft smile.

Suddenly the scene cuts: she is now wearing a dark blue Japanese sailor school uniform (seifuku) with white collar stripes and a red necktie. In the final shot, the camera pulls back slightly. She looks down at her hands which are now slightly claw-like or gloved in white fur, standing still in the middle of the flower-covered room.

Negative: blurry, low quality, distorted hands, extra fingers, watermark, text, ugly, deformed face, oversaturated

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 30-second photorealistic cinematic live-action sequence using the provided reference images.

# OVERALL STYLE

High-end cinematic streaming-series cinematography.
Photorealistic live action.
Bright natural summer daylight.
Beautiful English countryside.
Natural skin texture, realistic hair and fabric physics.
The scene begins peaceful and gradually becomes unsettling.
No fantasy CGI aesthetic.
No glowing magic.
No fog.
No dark horror lighting.
No dialogue.
No subtitles.

The storytelling must work visually without dialogue.

---

# SHOT 1 — PEACEFUL DISCOVERY

**0–4 seconds**

Wide cinematic establishing shot of the sunny meadow.

---# SHOT 2 — SHE PICKS IT

**4–8 seconds**

Smooth cinematic push-in toward the girl.

Cut to a detailed close-up of her hand approaching the flower.
Her fingers gently touch the stem.
She carefully plucks the flower.
The stem separates naturally.

Cut to her face.

She studies the flower with quiet fascination and a faint smile.
**The flower remains in her hand from this moment onward.**

-

## SHOT 3 — THE FLOWER RETURNS

**8–13 seconds**

Rear three-quarter tracking shot.
The girl stands and walks away through the meadow while holding the lavender flower.
Camera follows smoothly from behind at walking height.
After several steps, she slows and instinctively turns her head.
Camera performs a subtle rack focus past her shoulder toward the exact patch of soil where she picked the flower.
The soil moves slightly.
A fresh green stem emerges.
The same lavender spider lily slowly blooms again.
The girl is still several meters away.
She has not noticed it yet.
Hold for a brief beat.

---

## SHOT 4 — REALIZATION

**13–17 seconds**

The girl suddenly stops.
Camera makes a controlled push toward her from behind.
She turns around.
Over-the-shoulder shot reveals the regenerated flower standing exactly where she picked it.
She looks at the flower.
Then looks down at the identical flower still in her hand.
Her expression changes:
confusion → disbelief → unease.
Her breathing becomes slightly faster.
No dialogue.

---

## SHOT 5 THE RULE

**17–21 seconds**

She instinctively steps backward.
Her bare fingers brush through the grass.
Macro close-up of her fingertips touching the grass.
The grass bends naturally.
She pulls her hand away.
For one second, nothing happens.
Then a tiny lavender spider lily slowly emerges from the exact spot her fingers touched.
Cut immediately to her face.
Her eyes widen.
She understands that the meadow is reacting to her physical touch.
Keep the flower small and realistic.
No magical particles.

## SHOT 6  PANIC

**21–27 seconds**

She suddenly turns and runs.
Dynamic handheld rear tracking shot.
Camera follows closely behind as she sprints through the meadow.
Her dress and braids move naturally with her speed.
She repeatedly looks over her shoulder while running.
Her face shows genuine fear.
Every time her bare foot leaves the grass, a small lavender spider lily grows from that exact footprint approximately one second later.
One flower.
Then another.
Then another.
A trail rapidly forms behind her.
The flowers do NOT chase her.

They only grow from places she has already physically touched.

Brief side-tracking shot reveals the growing lavender trail across the meadow.

---

## SHOT 7  ROAD / HOPE

**27–30 seconds**

Hard cinematic transition to a wide roadside composition.

The girl bursts out of the meadow onto the grassy shoulder of a quiet English country road.
She stops abruptly.
Hands on knees.
Completely breathless.

She looks back toward the meadow.
A long trail of lavender flowers now marks her entire path.

Her face shows fear and disbelief.

A distant diesel engine is heard.

She turns toward the road.

A white European delivery truck appears around the bend.

Her expression changes from terror to desperate hope.

She raises one arm to stop it.

**Cut to black exactly as the truck begins braking.**
```

```
CAMERA:

DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld aesthetic, landscape framing. A gorgeous glamorous European fitness influencer films herself directly by hand in selfie-cam and first-person style.

Keep realistic hand shake, slightly crooked framing, delayed autofocus, awkward zoom-ins and zoom-outs, occasional motion blur, and small framing mistakes where part of her face briefly slips out of frame.

Mix handheld selfie footage with a few fixed external shots.

Important: never show her placing, adjusting, or setting up a camera. When switching from handheld footage to a fixed external angle, use a clean jump cut as if the camera had already been positioned before the shot began.

The camera itself must never appear on screen, including in mirror reflections.

LOOK:

Soft digital tape camcorder look with subtle vintage DV character.

Slight blur, faint tape noise, mild highlight bloom under gym lighting, subtle auto-exposure flicker, muted contrast, realistic skin texture, believable indoor lighting, realistic skin tones.

STYLE:

A realistic late-night gym vlog with a sexy Instagram-model vibe.

The tone is playful, confident, a little flirty, slightly breathless, and natural.

She is clearly attractive and charismatic, but the video should still feel like a believable self-recorded vlog, not a polished commercial.

Fast clean jump cuts, strong continuity, natural body language, no awkward dead moments.

CHARACTER:

An exceptionally beautiful glamorous European brunette woman in her mid-20s.

She has a highly photogenic face, large almond-shaped brown eyes, long lashes, full glossy lips, sculpted cheekbones, a refined nose, flawless lightly tanned glowing skin, and long thick glossy dark-brown hair tied in a high ponytail.

She looks like a high-end Instagram fitness and lifestyle influencer, not an ordinary gym visitor.

She has a slim, feminine, athletic hourglass figure.

She wears polished soft-glam makeup, a fitted stylish long-sleeve athletic crop top, high-waisted fitted leggings, clean white sneakers, and a towel around her neck or over one shoulder.

No jewelry.

SETTING:

A quiet modern gym late at night.

Mirror wall, dumbbell rack, flat bench, stretching mat, a water bottle on a bench, soft warm overhead lights, mostly empty space, calm atmosphere, no crowd, no trainer, no extra people.

IMPORTANT CONTINUITY RULES:

The same woman must remain fully consistent in every shot.

No face changes, no hairstyle changes, no outfit changes, no body changes.

No extra people appearing, not even in the mirror.

No duplicated limbs.

No broken hands.

No disappearing water bottle.

No broken gym equipment.

No impossible movements.

Mirror reflections must match her real position and must never show a camera.

No camera visible.

No camera setup shown.

Keep her beautiful, polished, and photogenic in every shot.

STORYBOARD:

30 seconds total, 10 cuts.

1. (~3s, arm's-length selfie)

She walks slowly through the gym holding the camera herself, slightly out of breath but smiling confidently.

Dialogue:
"Okay… late-night gym vlog."

2. (~3s, handheld pan across the room and back to her)

The camera drifts across the dumbbell rack, mirror wall, and empty gym floor, then returns to her face.

Dialogue:
"It's basically empty in here."

3. (~3s, mirror selfie angle)

She stops near the mirror, adjusts her ponytail, glances at herself, then smirks at the lens.

Dialogue:
"I look tired, but we're still doing this."

4. (~3s, fixed external medium shot)

Jump cut to a fixed shot.

She is already in position near the bench, picks up a pair of dumbbells, and gets ready.

No camera setup shown.

No dialogue.

5. (~3s, same fixed shot)

She performs a short controlled set of dumbbell shoulder presses with clean form, then lowers the weights and exhales with a smile.

Dialogue:
"That woke me up fast."

6. (~3s, handheld walking selfie)

She picks the vlog back up in selfie mode, towel over one shoulder, breathing a little harder as she walks a few steps.

Dialogue:
"Upper body done… and I'm already dying."

7. (~3s, tight handheld close-up)

She grabs the water bottle, takes a drink, lowers it, and exhales in relief.

Dialogue:
"Best part of the workout."

8. (~3s, fixed external wider shot facing the mirror)

Jump cut to a fixed shot.

She does a quick playful stretch, a small mirror pose, then laughs softly at herself.

Dialogue:
"A little stretch so I can pretend I'm disciplined."

9. (~3s, handheld close selfie)

Her cheeks are slightly flushed, she brushes back a loose strand of hair, smiling in a tired but satisfied way.

Dialogue:
"I always say quick workout… and then stay forever."

10. (~3s, arm's-length selfie ending)

She walks toward the exit, still filming herself, gives a tired little wave and a genuine smile.

Dialogue:
"Okay, I'm done. Good night."

FINAL INSTRUCTION:

The result must feel like a real self-recorded late-night gym vlog by a glamorous Instagram fitness influencer.

Prioritize realistic handheld motion, strong continuity, believable pacing, natural breathing, attractive appearance, and subtle imperfection.

Keep it sexy, polished, and realistic.

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 30-second, 16:9 horizontal, ultra-photorealistic live-action Japanese water obstacle competition show.

The visual style should resemble a late-2000s to early-2010s Japanese prime-time sports entertainment special, filmed as a real television broadcast with large audiences, practical machinery, stadium lighting, real water physics, professional ENG cameras, crane cameras, long lenses, tracking cameras and water-level cameras.

Everything must look physically built and practically filmed. No fantasy visuals, no CGI-looking environments, no artificial slow motion.

CHARACTER CONSISTENCY

Use the uploaded reference image as the ONLY visual identity reference for the female contestant.

Maintain exactly the same:

- face and facial structure
- hairstyle and hair color
- skin tone
- age appearance
- height
- body proportions
- physique
- clothing
- footwear
- overall appearance

The character must remain identical across every camera angle, including front, side, rear, low-angle, long-lens and close-up shots.

Her clothing must remain the same throughout the entire video, including after getting wet.

LOCATION CONTINUITY

The entire video takes place inside one continuous giant outdoor water-arena at night.

The course is arranged in this exact physical order:

START PLATFORM
→ FLOATING MAZE
→ ROTATING BRIDGE
→ GIANT WATER DROP

All obstacles must visibly exist in the same stadium environment.

Keep the audience stands, lighting towers, metal trusses, water pool and television production equipment spatially consistent.

0–3 SEC — CONFIDENT ENTRANCE

Begin with a close-up of the contestant's sneakers stepping onto the starting platform.

Cut naturally to:
legs → waist → upper body → face.

She looks toward the camera with a confident smile.

She gives a quick playful salute toward the audience.

The crowd reacts with loud applause.

She turns toward the course.

Her smile disappears.

She takes one deep breath and leans forward.

The starting buzzer suddenly sounds.

3–10 SEC — OBSTACLE ONE: FLOATING MAZE

The contestant immediately sprints toward a large floating maze made from interconnected soft platforms suspended just above the water.

3–4.5 SEC — CRANE WIDE SHOT

Reveal the entire obstacle.

Several floating platforms move independently because of water movement and mechanical cables underneath.

The contestant jumps onto the first platform.

The platform immediately tilts under her weight.

4.5–7 SEC — FRONT ENG TRACKING

She rapidly moves from platform to platform.

Every landing causes visible movement.

One platform unexpectedly rotates approximately 20–30 degrees.

She loses balance.

Her arms swing outward.

She quickly jumps onto the next platform instead of falling.

She laughs nervously for a split second and immediately continues.

7–9 SEC — LOW WATER CAMERA

The camera is almost at water level.

The contestant jumps between two moving platforms.

Her foot lands near the edge.

The platform tilts sharply.

She drops one knee onto the platform and catches herself with both hands.

Water splashes around her.

The audience screams.

She quickly pushes herself back up.

9–10 SEC — REAR TELEPHOTO

She makes one final jump and lands safely on the exit platform.

She immediately runs toward the second obstacle.

10–19 SEC — OBSTACLE TWO: ROTATING BRIDGE

A long narrow bridge stretches above the pool.

The bridge consists of several large cylindrical sections rotating at different speeds.

The contestant must cross while the cylinders continuously rotate underneath her feet.

10–12 SEC — HIGH WIDE SHOT

Show the full bridge and the water below.

She takes only a moment to judge the movement.

Then enters.

12–15 SEC — SIDE TRACKING SHOT

She runs across the first rotating section.

Her feet constantly adjust to the moving surface.

The second section rotates in the opposite direction.

She jumps onto it.

Her landing is unstable.

She takes two quick sideways steps.

The third section suddenly rotates faster.

She tries to accelerate.

15–17 SEC — FRONT ENG CLOSE SHOT

Her left foot slips.

Her body drops forward.

She catches a safety handrail with one hand.

Her legs swing briefly above the water.

The audience erupts.

She pulls herself back onto the rotating bridge.

She is visibly shocked but refuses to stop.

17–19 SEC — REAR FOLLOW CAMERA

She reaches the final rotating section.

She jumps forward.

Both feet land on the fixed platform.

She nearly falls again but regains balance.

She immediately runs toward the final obstacle.

19–27 SEC — FINAL OBSTACLE: GIANT WATER DROP

The final obstacle is a huge curved launch ramp leading to a narrow elevated landing platform suspended over the pool.

The contestant must sprint up the ramp and jump across a large gap.

19–21 SEC — LONG LENS FRONT SHOT

She runs directly toward the enormous ramp.

The audience becomes increasingly loud.

The camera compresses the distance, making the final jump appear extremely intimidating.

21–24 SEC — SIDE LOW CAMERA

She accelerates up the curved ramp.

Her final steps become longer.

She reaches the launch point.

She jumps.

Her body travels naturally through the air.

She stretches both arms toward the landing platform.

Her fingertips reach the platform edge.

For a moment it looks like she will succeed.

24–27 SEC — FAILED LANDING

Her hands catch the edge.

Her upper body pulls forward.

One knee reaches the platform.

She almost climbs onto it.

Then the platform unexpectedly tilts downward by a few degrees as part of the practical obstacle mechanism.

Her supporting foot loses contact.

Her body slides backward.

She tries to grab the edge again.

Her fingers slip.

She falls backward into the pool.

No exaggerated stunt motion.

Use realistic gravity and body momentum.

27–30 SEC — COMEDIC WATER ENDING

Use a water-level long shot as she hits the pool.

A large natural splash rises around her.

Cut immediately to a close-up.

She resurfaces with completely wet hair.

She looks toward the camera in disbelief.

She wipes water from her eyes.

She pauses.

Then gives a tiny annoyed smile as the crowd laughs and applauds.

End on her expression.

PHYSICAL REALISM

Every movement must follow:

movement → contact → force → body reaction → consequence.

Floating platforms react to body weight.

Rotating cylinders react naturally to foot placement.

Hands gripping rails must visibly support body weight.

The final jump must follow realistic trajectory, gravity and momentum.

Water must react naturally to every contact.

No teleportation.

No impossible balance.

No floating bodies.

No rubber-like human movement.

CAMERA LANGUAGE

Use authentic Japanese television sports coverage:

- crane wide shots
- handheld ENG cameras
- side tracking
- rear follow shots
- long-lens compression
- low water-level cameras
- close facial reactions
- quick broadcast cuts

Never use excessive cinematic slow motion.

Keep everything at natural real-time speed.

SOUND

Include:

- starting buzzer
- crowd cheering
- Japanese live commentary
- footsteps
- mechanical platform sounds
- rotating cylinder sounds
- water splashes
- contestant breathing
- crowd laughter
- impact sounds
- final splash

Commentary should react naturally to every major moment.

Music should gradually become more intense as the contestant approaches the final jump.

EMOTIONAL ARC

Confident
→ playful
→ nervous
→ unstable
→ determined
→ nearly successful
→ sudden failure
→ shocked
→ embarrassed
→ comedic ending

The contestant should look like a normal adult participant in a real entertainment competition, not a professional athlete.
```

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic cinematic Kathakali performance film set in Kerala, capturing the ritual, cultural depth, physical performance, costume, percussion and mythic storytelling power of the art form.

Begin in near-darkness with an extreme macro of a traditional oil lamp flame. Camera slowly pushes through the warm glow to reveal a Kathakali performer backstage: green facial makeup, white chutti, red accents, ornate headgear, layered costume, jewellery, painted eyes and highly detailed skin texture. Hands make final costume adjustments. The performer’s eyes open intensely.

Camera follows as he rises and walks toward a traditional Kerala performance space lit by rows of nilavilakku lamps. Musicians sit to one side with chenda, maddalam, chengila and ilathalam. The atmosphere feels sacred, live and physically real.

The performance begins with controlled stillness. Tight close-ups capture rapid eye movements, brow control, flared nostrils, facial expressions and precise mudras. Every gesture should feel like visual language, as if an epic story is being spoken without dialogue.

The rhythm builds.

Camera drops low and tracks laterally as the dancer begins stronger footwork, measured stomps, sweeping hand gestures and powerful body movements. The heavy skirt and layered costume respond realistically to each turn. Jewellery vibrates. Fine dust lifts from the floor. Oil-lamp flames react subtly to air movement.

Percussion intensifies.

Move into a faster sequence of expressive gestures, sharp eye changes and controlled spins. Camera transitions smoothly from macro facial detail to full-body movement, always keeping choreography readable.

At the emotional peak, the performer launches into a powerful mythic pose.

Enter extreme slow motion / bullet-time for a brief moment.

The costume flares outward, ornaments hang in motion, fingers hold a precise mudra, dust particles and lamp smoke float around him. Camera performs a smooth 180-degree orbit while the performer remains almost frozen except for a subtle eye movement toward camera.

All percussion drops into a low resonant pulse.

Then one massive CHENDA STRIKE snaps time back to full speed.

The dancer lands hard into the next movement. Full percussion returns. Footwork accelerates. Skirt rotates with force. Hands, eyes and body move in perfect rhythmic coordination.

Camera pulls wider to reveal the complete cultural tableau: performer at centre, musicians playing live, glowing lamps, Kerala architecture, dark audience silhouettes and warm firelit atmosphere.

For the final 5 seconds, the performance resolves into one unforgettable iconic stance.

The dancer plants both feet, torso powerful and upright, eyes locked forward, hands held in a strong meaningful mudra. Camera slowly pushes in as the percussion reaches its final phrase.

One final drum hit.

The performer becomes completely still.

Hold on the intense eyes and monumental costume for the final frame.

Cut to black.

VISUAL STYLE: hyper-real cinematic realism, authentic Kerala setting, warm oil-lamp lighting, deep shadows, natural skin and makeup texture, rich fabric detail, realistic jewellery, atmospheric smoke and dust, 24fps, natural motion blur, subtle film grain, strong depth, premium large-format cinema, restrained highlights, no glossy commercial look, no modern stage lighting, no neon, no fantasy-game styling.

CAMERA LANGUAGE: macro ritual details → controlled backstage glide → low-angle entrance → tight facial expression work → lateral dance tracking → brief bullet-time orbit → full-performance wide reveal → slow final push-in.

Ultra-photorealistic macro cinematic video, 8K, one continuous uninterrupted 30-second shot, no cuts or transitions.

A real adult man has been mysteriously reduced to 10 cm tall on a dining table during a lively household moment. He is a real human, Realistic skin, hair, clothing, anatomy, expressions, body weight, balance, and movement.

Maintain exact miniature scale until the end. From his perspective, plates look like arenas, forks like metal barriers, bread crumbs like boulders, fruit like giant structures, spilled juice like a dangerous flood, and a house cat and a normal-sized 10-year-old boy appear enormous.

Camera stays mostly at miniature eye level with handheld macro tracking, shallow depth of field, realistic focus breathing, cinematic motion blur, and strong foreground occlusion from cups, plates, fruit, utensils, and table objects. Every 3 to 4 seconds a new connected event happens, each more fun and chaotic than the last.

0-4 sec:

Camera rushes behind the miniature man as he runs across the wooden dining table between giant bread crumbs and a tipped spoon. The table vibrates as a huge house cat jumps onto a nearby chair, its giant paws and curious eyes entering frame.

4-8 sec:

The cat stretches one paw onto the table and bats at a rolling grape near the man. The grape rolls like a giant ball, forcing him to leap aside and climb onto a folded napkin while the cat's whiskers and nose loom close.

8-12 sec:

A normal-sized boy notices movement and leans over the table in amazement. As he reaches toward the tiny man to help, he accidentally nudges a glass, spilling juice across the table. A miniature wave rushes toward the man.

12-16 sec:

The tiny man grabs a fork and uses it as support while the juice flood carries fruit pieces, crumbs, and a cherry tomato past him. The cat, excited by the moving liquid and food, taps the table again, shaking everything.

16-20 sec:

The flood pushes the man onto a cracker like a raft. He slides past a giant plate and spins around a bowl of salad as the cat tries pawing at the floating cracker. The boy follows alongside, carefully trying not to make things worse.

20-24 sec:

The cracker raft crashes into a banana slice. The man tumbles onto a giant strawberry. The cat's tail sweeps across the table like a moving wall, almost knocking him off. The boy places his open hand nearby, trying to rescue him.

24-27 sec:

The miniature man jumps from the strawberry to the boy's palm, but the cat playfully pounces at the moving hand. The boy pulls back quickly, and the man slides down onto the center of the table beside a glowing decorative object or small device.

27-30 sec CLIMAX:

The object suddenly emits a bright blue-gold energy beam around the miniature man. Dust, crumbs, droplets, and napkin fibers swirl upward. He smoothly grows from 10 cm to normal adult size in one continuous transformation. As he grows, the giant-looking cat, boy, plates, crumbs, and fruit return to ordinary scale. Camera pulls back to reveal the restored man standing on the dining room floor beside the astonished boy, while the cat stares curiously at him. Final image: the once terrifying grape sits harmlessly near his shoe as the glowing object gives one last pulse.

Rendering requirements:

Sony A1, 100mm macro lens look for miniature moments, HDR, warm indoor daylight, realistic reflections on glass and cutlery, accurate cat fur, whiskers and movement, believable liquid physics, flexible napkin motion, realistic contact shadows, scale consistency, gravity, inertia, and detailed table textures. Fast, fun, readable action with clear cause and effect.
```

### 30秒电影感奇幻训练，10个镜头，16–18个不同机位，节奏快速但动作清晰易读。

#### 📝 Prompt

```
30秒电影感奇幻训练，10个镜头，16–18个不同机位，节奏快速但动作清晰易读。

角色锁定：
 Boy / 小骑士： 一名年轻的见习骑士，造型简洁、独特，使用木制弓箭，背后带箭袋。
 Fox / 狐狸： 一只大型、修长、运动型的奇幻狐狸，身体敏捷，四肢修长，耳朵和尾巴富有表现力。体型足够大，可以让Boy站在背上完成各种动作。
 Master / 师父： 一位年老而严厉的训练师，高瘦身材，灰色头发，短灰胡须，穿深炭灰与深棕色的破旧奇幻服装，气质冷静而威严。Shot 1–9完全不出现，只在Shot 10第一次亮相。
全程保持相同角色设计。禁止克隆角色。Boy全程双脚不能接触地面。
沙漠训练场锁定：
全程保持同一个沙漠训练场：干燥沙地、稀疏黄色草丛、远处岩石山丘、带沙尘的风。整个训练路线始终连续向前，沿途设置木制假人、射箭靶、蛇形绕杆、低矮横梁、门架和跳跃围栏。禁止切换环境。
核心动作：
Boy + Fox必须像一个经过高度训练的整体一样行动。Fox始终向前奔跑，并主动完成冲刺、倾斜转向、侧步、低伏、滑行、跳跃、加速。Boy很少坐下；他持续利用Fox产生的动量完成站立、下蹲、平衡、旋转、翻腾和射箭。

SHOT 1 | 0–3秒 — Hook
Fox高速向前冲刺。Boy高高站在它背上。Fox低伏穿过低矮横梁 → Boy同时迅速贴伏在Fox背上 → 穿过障碍 → 立刻重新站起。
低机位正面 → 侧面跟拍。

SHOT 2 | 3–6秒 — Slalom
Fox在绕杆之间高速蛇形穿梭，身体大幅向左/右倾斜转向。Boy通过深度侧倾、换脚、单腿平衡以及带有Hip-Hop节奏感的身体动作进行反向平衡。
正面 → 顶视角。
SHOT 3 | 6–9秒 — 草原骑射
Fox向目标方向倾斜转向。Boy以古代草原骑手姿态站立 → 上半身大幅旋转 → 拉弓 → 向侧面射箭 → 正中靶心。Fox的运动自然将Boy重新带回面向前方的状态。
3/4侧面跟拍。

SHOT 4 | 9–12秒 — 前空翻
Fox压低身体蓄力并起跳 → Boy利用Fox的爆发力完全离开它的背部 → 向前空翻。Fox始终在Boy下方继续向前奔跑 → Boy精准落回正在高速移动的Fox背上。
侧面 → 低机位慢动作。
SHOT 5 | 12–15秒 — 侧挂射击
Fox侧步绕过木杆 → 动量将Boy甩向身体侧面，几乎水平悬挂在Fox旁边。Boy保持悬挂姿势向木制假人射箭 → Fox立即向相反方向倾斜转向 → 利用反向动量帮助Boy摆回背上。
低机位侧面跟拍。

SHOT 6 | 15–18秒 — 流畅组合
Fox低伏 → Boy贴伏。Fox抬起身体 → Boy站起。Fox斜向滑过门架 → Boy利用这股动量完成换脚 → 身体旋转 → 转为背向前进方向的站姿。
动态跟拍。
SHOT 7 | 18–21秒 — 空中射击
Fox进行一次距离很长但高度较低的跳跃，同时Boy向更高处跃起。两者在空中明显分离。Boy空中旋转180° → 拉弓 → 射箭 → 正中靶心 → 再旋转回来 → 几乎与Fox落地同时精准落回Fox背上。
宽幅侧面 → 低机位空中视角。
SHOT 8 | 21–24秒 — 舞蹈组合
Fox：蛇形移动 → 倾斜转向 → 侧步 → 加速。
Boy：下蹲 → 站起 → 单腿平衡 → 快速步法 → 旋转 → 背向骑乘 → 向后方已经经过的目标射箭 → 正中靶心。
后方跟拍 → 高位3/4视角。
SHOT 9 | 24–28秒 — 终极组合
Boy + Fox同时压低身体蓄力 → 同步爆发起跳。Fox进行距离很长的低空跳跃；Boy则高高飞起 → 空翻 → 在空中舒展开身体 → 空中拉弓射箭 → 正中靶心。Fox始终继续向前，从Boy下方穿行 → Boy开始下降 → 两者同步落地 → Boy立刻在Fox背上骄傲地站起。
低机位正面 → 宽幅侧面 → 跟随箭矢 → 后方跟拍。

SHOT 10 | 28–30秒 — 师父首次亮相
Master第一次出现，独自站在终点线上，深色服装与明亮沙漠形成强烈反差。Fox在他身旁减速。Boy骄傲地站在Fox背上，期待得到称赞。Master看向Boy → 再看向Fox → 平静地指向训练路线的起点。Boy失望地垂下肩膀；Fox也耷拉下耳朵。切黑。
严格动作逻辑：
Fox压低身体 → 将Boy送起；
Fox倾斜转向 → Boy反向平衡；
Fox侧步 → Boy侧挂；
Fox起跳 → Boy跳得更高；
Fox调整步幅 → Boy精准落回背上。
两者必须持续协同配合，绝不能各自独立完成动作。始终向前推进。禁止循环动作、瞬移、重置训练路线，禁止为了表演特技而停下来。
音频：
无音乐。仅保留狐狸奔跑脚步声、沙漠风声、扬尘声、布料与皮革摩擦声、跳跃破风声、弓弦声、箭矢飞行声以及击中木制目标的撞击声。
```

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 30-second real-time Japanese obstacle-course sports TV broadcast set at a huge outdoor venue at night, with a deep competition pool, stadium lights, steel structures, and packed audience stands. The style should resemble late-2000s/early-2010s Japanese terrestrial sports entertainment: realistic ENG/crane/wire/rail/telephoto/low-water cameras, natural pans and zooms, slight handheld shake, early-HD softness, TV compression, realistic colors, live audience reactions, Japanese sports commentary, obstacle sounds, footsteps, water ambience, start/end buzzers, and tense instrumental music. No slow motion, replay, time skips, cinematic speed ramps, logos, subtitles, scoreboard, timer, watermark, or unnecessary text.

The challenger is the Aqua Breeze girl from the reference image: she must remain the exact same 2D anime-style girl physically integrated into the realistic live-action environment, never a human actress, cosplayer, or different 3D character. Preserve her blue-green eyes, long aqua twin-tails, fair skin, slim athletic proportions, white-and-aqua summer outfit, frilled bikini-style top, aqua skirt, tropical flower, starfish accessory, jewelry, and overall identity throughout. No identity, face, body, hairstyle, costume, accessory, or color drift.

Obstacle order is fixed: Quad Step Bridge → Rolling Log Run → Reverse Fishbone → Warped Wall. 0–3s: knee-up ENG shot pushes toward her face at the start platform; she smiles, acknowledges the cheering audience, then focuses on the course. Japanese announcer: 「さあ、アクアブリーズの挑戦者、スタートラインに立ちました！」 3–4s: buzzer, wide crane shot, she immediately sprints forward. 「スタートした！」

4–8s: Quad Step Bridge. She quickly makes diagonal jumps across four angled floating platforms, briefly landing near one edge but correcting instantly and clearing it smoothly. Tracking cameras follow her continuously. 「まずは四連ジャンプ！ 軽やかに渡っていく！」

8–13s: Rolling Log Run. She runs across the rotating cylindrical log above the pool. Her body sways, one foot slips sharply, the audience gasps, but she spreads her arms, recovers, jumps from the end, and lands safely. 「続いてローリングログ！ 足元が回る！」 then 「おっと滑った！ しかし耐えた！」

13–22s: Reverse Fishbone. She immediately enters the narrow path while large rotating bars move against her direction. She ducks under one, twists past another, jumps another, and keeps moving. From a low water-level camera, a bar nearly hits her leg; she lifts it, lands near the platform edge, slips toward the water and almost falls. The audience screams 「危ない！」 She desperately swings her arms, regains balance, accelerates, ducks under the final bar, jumps to the exit platform, and clears it. 「危ない！ しかし残った！」 The audience erupts.

22–30s: Warped Wall. Exhausted but determined, she sprints toward the huge curved wall. First attempt: she runs up, reaches with her right hand, misses the top by only a few centimeters, and slides down realistically. She immediately turns back, breathing heavily, as the audience shouts 「いけーーー！」 Second attempt: she uses all remaining strength, runs higher, and her fingertips touch the top edge. She almost grabs it but cannot hold on; her fingers slip and she slides down exactly as the end buzzer sounds. 「届くか……触った！ しかし掴みきれない！」 Final ENG shot: hands on knees, breathing heavily, she looks up with regret, gives a small bittersweet smile, then lightly bows to the audience as the entire venue applauds. 「あとほんのわずか！ 惜しい挑戦でした！」

Keep all action physically believable and easy to follow. Never lose sight of the challenger. Her hair and skirt react naturally to movement, but her design never changes. Maintain the fixed course layout and clear arc: smooth opening clear → brief balance slip → dangerous middle crisis → heartbreaking final failure.
```
